bank + --lex restriction concentrating resonance-weighted picks onto ~15-18 subjects.
David's fix proposal: drop the NOUS/HERMES wordmark requirement, go back to --sref random,
"play around for a long time with different prompt permutations of various aesthetic and
art history explorations." Also: "I thought the blender was bigger" -- correct diagnosis,
found via agent-swarm research that a SEPARATE, larger, already-completed engine (madlib
v1/v2, 1,255 prompts, COMPLETE 2026-07-06) had a 102-subject bank, 28 camera/lighting
modifiers, 36-artist fine-art bank, and real museum-artwork --sref URL packs (NGA 75
artists + multi-museum 1,824 artists across AIC/CMA/MET/Europeana) that were never wired
into blender.py. Built blender.py v3: merged all of the above in (32 net-new fine-art
INFLUENCE entries after dedup), added a THEMES config layer (nous-branded / art-history-
madlib / colorist-typography / off-brief-random), ported the madlib audit's coverage-
guaranteed affine-permutation picker (fixes the actual root cause of the monotony -- see
PROMPT-ENGINE-AUDIT.md), and wired sref_pack.py for real artwork --sref pulls. Full design
notes: reference-mj-sref-museum-pack-gotchas memory.

off-brief-random (unbranded, SUBJECTS_LARGE 102-item bank, --sref random random stacked),
